Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the fossils structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 799. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 767 and converges at rate O(1/n^4).

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by fossils generators. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 767. QED.
